null;\n33\t  stripeCustomerId?: string | null;\n34\t  stripeSessionId?: string | null;\n35\t}\n36\t\n37\texport interface AccountKeyRow {\n38\t  id: string;\n39\t  key: string;\n40\t  email: string;\n41\t  userId: string;\n42\t  status: string;\n43\t  polarLicenseKeyId: string | null;\n44\t  polarCustomerId: string | null;\n45\t  stripeCustomerId: string | null;\n46\t  stripeSessionId: string | null;\n47\t  createdAt: Date;\n48\t}\n49\t\n50\texport type CreditGrantSourceType =\n51\t  | \"license_bundle\"\n52\t  | \"polar_bundle\"\n53\t  | \"internal_bundle\"\n54\t  | \"admin_license_bundle\"\n55\t  | \"stripe_credit_pack\"\n56\t  | \"admin_manual\"\n57\t  | \"legacy_unknown\";\n58\t\n59\texport interface StripeCreditGrantInsert {\n60\t  eventId: string;\n61\t  eventType: string;\n62\t  stripeObjectId: string;\n63\t  userId: string;\n64\t  creditAmount: number;\n65\t  sourceType?: CreditGrantSourceType;\n66\t  sourceId?: string;\n67\t}\n68\t\n69\texport interface CreditGrantInsert {\n70\t  userId: string;\n71\t  amount: number;\n72\t  sourceType: CreditGrantSourceType;\n73\t  sourceId: string;\n74\t}\n75\t\n76\texport interface CreditGrantRefund {\n77\t  sourceType: CreditGrantSourceType;\n78\t  sourceId: string;\n79\t}\n80\t\n81\texport interface UserResult {\n82\t  id: string;\n83\t  email: string;\n84\t}\n85\t\n86\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n87\t// Helpers\n88\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n89\t\n90\tfunction drizzleAccountKeyToRow(row: typeof accountKeys.$inferSelect): AccountKeyRow {\n91\t  return {\n92\t    id: row.id,\n93\t    key: row.key,\n94\t    email: row.email,\n95\t    userId: row.userId,\n96\t    status: row.status,\n97\t    polarLicenseKeyId: row.polarLicenseKeyId,\n98\t    polarCustomerId: row.polarCustomerId,\n99\t    stripeCustomerId: row.stripeCustomerId,\n100\t    stripeSessionId: row.stripeSessionId,\n101\t    createdAt: row.createdAt,\n102\t  };\n103\t}\n104\t\n105\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n106\t// License Keys\n107\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n108\t\n109\texport async function insertAccountKey(data: AccountKeyInsert): Promise<AccountKeyRow | null> {\n110\t  const polarLicenseKeyId = data.polarLicenseKeyId ?? null;\n111\t  const insert = db\n112\t    .insert(accountKeys)\n113\t    .values({\n114\t      key: data.key,\n115\t      email: data.email,\n116\t      userId: data.userId,\n117\t      status: data.status ?? \"granted\",\n118\t      polarLicenseKeyId,\n119\t      polarCustomerId: data.polarCustomerId ?? null,\n120\t      stripeCustomerId: data.stripeCustomerId ?? null,\n121\t      stripeSessionId: data.stripeSessionId ?? null,\n122\t    });\n123\t\n124\t  // When importing a Polar license, the unique index on polar_license_key_id\n125\t  // (idx_license_keys_polar_license_key_id) is the authoritative dedupe guard:\n126\t  // if a concurrent import already inserted a row for this Polar key, skip the\n127\t  // insert and return the existing row instead of failing or creating a\n128\t  // duplicate. The read-then-write check in importLicenseFromPolar handles the\n129\t  // common case; this makes the rare race deterministic.\n130\t  const [row] = polarLicenseKeyId\n131\t    ? await insert\n132\t        .onConflictDoNothing({ target: accountKeys.polarLicenseKeyId })\n133\t        .returning()\n134\t    : await insert.returning();\n135\t\n136\t  if (!row) {\n137\t    return polarLicenseKeyId\n138\t      ? await findAccountByPolarLicenseKeyId(polarLicenseKeyId)\n139\t      : null;\n140\t  }\n141\t  return drizzleAccountKeyToRow(row);\n142\t}\n143\t\n144\texport async function updateAccountKey(\n145\t  id: string,\n146\t  updates: Partial<Pick<AccountKeyInsert, \"status\" | \"stripeCustomerId\" | \"email\">>\n147\t): Promise<void> {\n148\t  const values: Record<string, unknown> = {};\n149\t  if (updates.status !== undefined) values.status = updates.status;\n150\t  if (updates.stripeCustomerId !== undefined) values.stripeCustomerId = updates.stripeCustomerId;\n151\t  if (updates.email !== undefined) values.email = updates.email;\n152\t\n153\t  await db.update(accountKeys).set(values).where(eq(accountKeys.id, id));\n154\t}\n155\t\n156\texport async function findAccountByKey(key: string): Promise<AccountKeyRow | null> {\n157\t  const row = await db.query.accountKeys.findFirst({\n158\t    where: eq(accountKeys.key, key.trim()),\n159\t  });\n160\t  return row ? drizzleAccountKeyToRow(row) : null;\n161\t}\n162\t\n163\texport async function findAccountById(id: string): Promise<AccountKeyRow | null> {\n164\t  const row = await db.query.accountKeys.findFirst({\n165\t    where: eq(accountKeys.id, id),\n166\t  });\n167\t  return row ? drizzleAccountKeyToRow(row) : null;\n168\t}\n169\t\n170\texport async function findAccountByStripeSession(sessionId: string): Promise<AccountKeyRow | null> {\n171\t  const row = await db.query.accountKeys.findFirst({\n172\t    where: eq(accountKeys.stripeSessionId, sessionId),\n173\t  });\n174\t  return row ? drizzleAccountKeyToRow(row) : null;\n175\t}\n176\t\n177\texport async function findAccountByPolarLicenseKeyId(\n178\t  polarLicenseKeyId: string\n179\t): Promise<AccountKeyRow | null> {\n180\t  const row = await db.query.accountKeys.findFirst({\n181\t    where: eq(accountKeys.polarLicenseKeyId, polarLicenseKeyId),\n182\t  });\n183\t  return row ? drizzleAccountKeyToRow(row) : null;\n184\t}\n185\t\n186\texport async function getAccountKeysByEmail(email: string): Promise<AccountKeyRow[]> {\n187\t  const rows = await db.query.accountKeys.findMany({\n188\t    where: eq(accountKeys.email, email.toLowerCase()),\n189\t    orderBy: [desc(accountKeys.createdAt)],\n190\t  });\n191\t  return rows.map(drizzleAccountKeyToRow);\n192\t}\n193\t\n194\texport async function getAllAccountKeysForAdmin(limit = 1000): Promise<AccountKeyRow[]> {\n195\t  const rows = await db.query.accountKeys.findMany({\n196\t    orderBy: [desc(accountKeys.createdAt)],\n197\t    limit,\n198\t  });\n199\t  return rows.map(drizzleAccountKeyToRow);\n200\t}\n201\t\n202\t/**\n203\t * Distinct normalized emails that hold at least one *granted* Account Key (from\n204\t * the internal bundle or a paid purchase). Powers the ACS admin backfill, which\n205\t * reconciles its claim flag against emails that already have a key. Read-only.\n206\t */\n207\texport async function getGrantedEmails(): Promise<string[]> {\n208\t  const rows = await db\n209\t    .selectDistinct({ email: accountKeys.email })\n210\t    .from(accountKeys)\n211\t    .where(eq(accountKeys.status, \"granted\"));\n212\t  return rows\n213\t    .map((r) => r.email.toLowerCase().trim())\n214\t    .filter((email) => email.length > 0);\n215\t}\n216\t\n217\texport async function searchAccountKeysByEmail(\n218\t  email: string,\n219\t  limit = 1000\n220\t): Promise<Array<AccountKeyRow & { credits: number }>> {\n221\t  const rows = await db.query.accountKeys.findMany({\n222\t    where: ilike(accountKeys.email, `%${email}%`),\n223\t    orderBy: [desc(accountKeys.createdAt)],\n224\t    limit,\n225\t  });\n226\t  const licenses = rows.map(drizzleAccountKeyToRow);\n227\t  return attachPooledCredits(licenses);\n228\t}\n229\t\n230\t// Credits granted with each internal-bundle Account Key mint. $10 at the\n231\t// 1000-credits-per-dollar rate. This is the ACS member perk; only brand-new\n232\t// emails receive it (an email that already has a granted key gets no top-up).\n233\tconst INTERNAL_BUNDLE_CREDITS = 10000;\n234\t\n235\t/**\n236\t * Mint a fresh internal-bundle license for an email: generate a collision-free\n237\t * key, ensure the user exists, insert the license as \"granted\", and grant the\n238\t * standard 10,000-credit ($10) internal bundle.\n239\t *\n240\t * This is the single source of truth for the internal mint flow. Its only\n241\t * caller is the grant-license endpoint (driven by the ACS claim flow), which\n242\t * mints only when the email has no granted key. licenses-for-email is read-only\n243\t * and never mints. Throws on any failure.\n244\t */\n245\texport async function provisionAccountKeyForEmail(email: string): Promise<AccountKeyRow> {\n246\t  const normalizedEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();\n247\t\n248\t  // Generate a unique license key with collision check\n249\t  let key = \"\";\n250\t  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {\n251\t    key = generateLicenseKey();\n252\t    const collision = await findAccountByKey(key);\n253\t    if (!collision) break;\n254\t    if (i === 4) {\n255\t      throw new Error(\"Failed to generate unique license key\");\n256\t    }\n257\t  }\n258\t\n259\t  const name = normalizedEmail.split(\"@\")[0];\n260\t  const userResult = await getOrCreateUser(normalizedEmail, { name });\n261\t  if (!userResult) {\n262\t    throw new Error(\"Failed to create user\");\n263\t  }\n264\t\n265\t  const license = await insertAccountKey({\n266\t    key,\n267\t    email: normalizedEmail,\n268\t    userId: userResult.id,\n269\t    status: \"granted\",\n270\t  });\n271\t  if (!license) {\n272\t    throw new Error(\"Failed to insert license key\");\n273\t  }\n274\t\n275\t  await grantCreditLot({\n276\t    userId: license.userId,\n277\t    amount: INTERNAL_BUNDLE_CREDITS,\n278\t    sourceType: \"internal_bundle\",\n279\t    sourceId: license.id,\n280\t  });\n281\t\n282\t  return license;\n283\t}\n284\t\n285\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n286\t// Credit Balances and Grants\n287\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n288\t\n289\t// A handle that can run queries: either the pooled `db` connection or a\n290\t// transaction handle. Used so balance reads can join the caller's transaction\n291\t// instead of opening a second connection (which sees a stale snapshot and ties\n292\t// up the pool while row locks are held).\n293\ttype DbExecutor = typeof db | Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0];\n294\t\n295\t// A grant still backs spendable balance when it has no expiry or hasn't expired\n296\t// yet. This is the single source of that rule for the raw-SQL credit queries\n297\t// below (balance read, spend, clawback) — keep it here so expiry semantics live\n298\t// in one place. The Drizzle-builder variant (using creditGrants.expiresAt) is\n299\t// expressed inline where the query is built with the query builder.\n300\tconst ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY = sql`(expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > now())`;\n301\t\n302\t/**\n303\t * Authoritative spendable balance: the SUM of remaining_amount across a\n304\t * license's still-active grants. The credit_grants rows are the source of\n305\t * truth; credit_balances is only a denormalized cache of this value. Reading\n306\t * via the passed executor lets callers inside a FOR UPDATE transaction get a\n307\t * consistent, locked view rather than a stale snapshot from the outer pool.\n308\t */\n309\tasync function getActiveGrantsTotal(\n310\t  executor: DbExecutor,\n311\t  userId: string\n312\t): Promise<number> {\n313\t  const result = await executor.execute<{ total: string | null }>(sql`\n314\t    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(remaining_amount), 0) AS total\n315\t    FROM credit_grants\n316\t    WHERE user_id = ${userId}\n317\t      AND status = 'active'\n318\t      AND remaining_amount > 0\n319\t      AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n320\t  `);\n321\t  return Number(result.rows[0]?.total ?? 0);\n322\t}\n323\t\n324\t/**\n325\t * Force the credit_balances cache to equal the authoritative grant total,\n326\t * reconciling any drift between the two. Returns the reconciled balance. This\n327\t * is the single place that heals divergence (e.g. a legacy balance row with no\n328\t * matching grants, or a partially-applied transaction) so the cache can never\n329\t * permanently show credits the grants can't back, or vice versa.\n330\t */\n331\tasync function reconcileCreditBalance(\n332\t  tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0],\n333\t  userId: string\n334\t): Promise<number> {\n335\t  const total = await getActiveGrantsTotal(tx, userId);\n336\t  await tx\n337\t    .insert(creditBalances)\n338\t    .values({ userId, balance: total.toString() })\n339\t    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n340\t      target: creditBalances.userId,\n341\t      set: {\n342\t        balance: total.toString(),\n343\t        updatedAt: new Date(),\n344\t      },\n345\t    });\n346\t  return total;\n347\t}\n348\t\n349\tasync function incrementCreditBalance(\n350\t  tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0],\n351\t  userId: string,\n352\t  amount: number\n353\t): Promise<number> {\n354\t  const [row] = await tx\n355\t    .insert(creditBalances)\n356\t    .values({ userId, balance: amount.toString() })\n357\t    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n358\t      target: creditBalances.userId,\n359\t      set: {\n360\t        balance: sql`${creditBalances.balance} + ${amount}`,\n361\t        updatedAt: new Date(),\n362\t      },\n363\t    })\n364\t    .returning({ balance: creditBalances.balance });\n365\t\n366\t  return Number(row.balance);\n367\t}\n368\t\n369\t// Every credit grant expires one year after it is created (OpenRouter model):\n370\t// the ToS reserves the right to expire unused credits 365 days after purchase.\n371\t// Stamped here so it covers every insert path (paid packs, minted keys, admin\n372\t// grants); enforcement is the lazy `expires_at > now()` filter on reads/spends.\n373\tconst CREDIT_GRANT_TTL_MS = 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n374\t\n375\tasync function grantCreditLotInTransaction(\n376\t  tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0],\n377\t  data: CreditGrantInsert\n378\t): Promise<{ status: \"processed\" | \"duplicate\"; balance: number }> {\n379\t  const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + CREDIT_GRANT_TTL_MS);\n380\t  const [grantRow] = await tx\n381\t    .insert(creditGrants)\n382\t    .values({\n383\t      userId: data.userId,\n384\t      sourceType: data.sourceType,\n385\t      sourceId: data.sourceId,\n386\t      originalAmount: data.amount.toString(),\n387\t      remainingAmount: data.amount.toString(),\n388\t      refundedAmount: \"0\",\n389\t      status: \"active\",\n390\t      expiresAt,\n391\t    })\n392\t    .onConflictDoNothing({\n393\t      target: [creditGrants.sourceType, creditGrants.sourceId],\n394\t    })\n395\t    .returning({ id: creditGrants.id });\n396\t\n397\t  if (!grantRow) {\n398\t    // Read inside the transaction so the balance reflects the locked, in-tx\n399\t    // view rather than a stale snapshot from a second pooled connection.\n400\t    const balance = await getActiveGrantsTotal(tx, data.userId);\n401\t    return { status: \"duplicate\", balance };\n402\t  }\n403\t\n404\t  const balance = await incrementCreditBalance(tx, data.userId, data.amount);\n405\t  return { status: \"processed\", balance };\n406\t}\n407\t\n408\texport async function grantCreditLot(\n409\t  data: CreditGrantInsert\n410\t): Promise<{ status: \"processed\" | \"duplicate\"; balance: number }> {\n411\t  return db.transaction((tx) => grantCreditLotInTransaction(tx, data));\n412\t}\n413\t\n414\texport async function grantCreditsForStripeEvent(\n415\t  data: StripeCreditGrantInsert\n416\t): Promise<\"processed\" | \"duplicate\"> {\n417\t  const insertedEvent = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n418\t    const [eventRow] = await tx\n419\t      .insert(stripeProcessedEvents)\n420\t      .values({\n421\t        eventId: data.eventId,\n422\t        eventType: data.eventType,\n423\t        stripeObjectId: data.stripeObjectId,\n424\t      })\n425\t      .onConflictDoNothing({ target: stripeProcessedEvents.stripeObjectId })\n426\t      .returning({ eventId: stripeProcessedEvents.eventId });\n427\t\n428\t    if (!eventRow) {\n429\t      return null;\n430\t    }\n431\t\n432\t    await grantCreditLotInTransaction(tx, {\n433\t      userId: data.userId,\n434\t      amount: data.creditAmount,\n435\t      sourceType: data.sourceType ?? \"stripe_credit_pack\",\n436\t      sourceId: data.sourceId ?? data.stripeObjectId,\n437\t    });\n438\t\n439\t    return eventRow;\n440\t  });\n441\t\n442\t  return insertedEvent ? \"processed\" : \"duplicate\";\n443\t}\n444\t\n445\texport async function refundCreditGrant(\n446\t  data: CreditGrantRefund\n447\t): Promise<{ status: \"processed\" | \"duplicate\"; refundedAmount: number }> {\n448\t  return db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n449\t    // Resolve the refunded purchase by provenance. We deliberately do NOT filter\n450\t    // on remaining_amount > 0: a fully-consumed pack still has to be clawed back,\n451\t    // otherwise spending the pack first (so a refund can't hand back unused paid\n452\t    // credits — see spendCreditGrantsByProvenance / #872) would let a refund of a\n453\t    // fully-spent pack reclaim nothing, leaving the user with the paid usage for\n454\t    // free PLUS the cash refund (the inverse money-loss bug).\n455\t    const target = await tx.execute<{\n456\t      id: string;\n457\t      user_id: string;\n458\t      original_amount: string;\n459\t      refunded_amount: string;\n460\t    }>(sql`\n461\t      SELECT id, user_id, original_amount, refunded_amount\n462\t      FROM credit_grants\n463\t      WHERE source_type = ${data.sourceType}\n464\t        AND source_id = ${data.sourceId}\n465\t      FOR UPDATE\n466\t    `);\n467\t\n468\t    const grant = target.rows[0];\n469\t    if (!grant) {\n470\t      return { status: \"duplicate\", refundedAmount: 0 };\n471\t    }\n472\t\n473\t    // The amount this refund reclaims is the grant's full purchased value that\n474\t    // has not already been refunded. A refunded grant (refunded_amount already\n475\t    // covers original_amount) is a no-op / duplicate webhook delivery.\n476\t    const originalAmount = Number(grant.original_amount);\n477\t    const alreadyRefunded = Number(grant.refunded_amount);\n478\t    const clawback = originalAmount - alreadyRefunded;\n479\t    if (clawback <= 0) {\n480\t      return { status: \"duplicate\", refundedAmount: 0 };\n481\t    }\n482\t\n483\t    // A full refund must remove the FULL purchased value from the license's\n484\t    // spendable balance, regardless of which grant happened to be spent for the\n485\t    // matching usage. Clamped at the license's current active total so a user who\n486\t    // already burned the credits doesn't go negative, but no value escapes:\n487\t    //   spend order + refund clawback are reconciled against the SAME running\n488\t    //   total, so neither bundle-first nor pack-first ordering can leak money.\n489\t    let toClawback = Math.min(\n490\t      clawback,\n491\t      await getActiveGrantsTotal(tx, grant.user_id)\n492\t    );\n493\t\n494\t    // Draw the clawback down from the license's active, unexpired grants,\n495\t    // starting with the refunded grant itself, then the same oldest-first order\n496\t    // used when spending (soonest-to-expire first). The clawback total is\n497\t    // clamped at the license's current active balance, and both spend and\n498\t    // clawback reconcile against that same running total, so no ordering can\n499\t    // leak money. Expired grants are skipped: they no longer back any spendable\n500\t    // balance, so clawing them back would remove value that was never available.\n501\t    const drawdown = await tx.execute<{ id: string; remaining_amount: string }>(sql`\n502\t      SELECT id, remaining_amount\n503\t      FROM credit_grants\n504\t      WHERE user_id = ${grant.user_id}\n505\t        AND remaining_amount > 0\n506\t        AND status = 'active'\n507\t        AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n508\t      ORDER BY\n509\t        CASE WHEN id = ${grant.id} THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,\n510\t        expires_at ASC,\n511\t        created_at ASC,\n512\t        id\n513\t      FOR UPDATE\n514\t    `);\n515\t\n516\t    for (const row of drawdown.rows) {\n517\t      if (toClawback <= 0) break;\n518\t      const rowRemaining = Number(row.remaining_amount);\n519\t      const deduction = Math.min(rowRemaining, toClawback);\n520\t      if (deduction <= 0) continue;\n521\t      const newRemaining = rowRemaining - deduction;\n522\t      await tx\n523\t        .update(creditGrants)\n524\t        .set({\n525\t          remainingAmount: newRemaining.toString(),\n526\t          status: newRemaining === 0 ? \"spent\" : \"active\",\n527\t          updatedAt: new Date(),\n528\t        })\n529\t        .where(eq(creditGrants.id, row.id));\n530\t      toClawback -= deduction;\n531\t    }\n532\t\n533\t    // Mark the refunded grant as refunded and record the full reclaimed value on\n534\t    // it (even when the credits were physically drawn from other grants), so the\n535\t    // grant's lifetime is correct and a duplicate refund webhook is a no-op.\n536\t    await tx\n537\t      .update(creditGrants)\n538\t      .set({\n539\t        remainingAmount: \"0\",\n540\t        refundedAmount: (alreadyRefunded + clawback).toString(),\n541\t        status: \"refunded\",\n542\t        updatedAt: new Date(),\n543\t      })\n544\t      .where(eq(creditGrants.id, grant.id));\n545\t\n546\t    // Reconcile the cache to the authoritative grant total, healing any drift.\n547\t    await reconcileCreditBalance(tx, grant.user_id);\n548\t    return {\n549\t      status: \"processed\",\n550\t      refundedAmount: clawback,\n551\t    };\n552\t  });\n553\t}\n554\t\n555\texport async function spendCreditGrantsByProvenance(\n556\t  userId: string,\n557\t  amount: number\n558\t): Promise<{ balance: number; deductedAmount: number }> {\n559\t  return db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n560\t    const result = await tx.execute<{ id: string; remaining_amount: string }>(sql`\n561\t      SELECT id, remaining_amount\n562\t      FROM credit_grants\n563\t      WHERE user_id = ${userId}\n564\t        AND remaining_amount > 0\n565\t        AND status = 'active'\n566\t        AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n567\t      -- Spend OLDEST-FIRST: soonest-to-expire grants are consumed before grants\n568\t      -- that still have time on them (and never-expiring grants last), so a user\n569\t      -- naturally burns down credits before they lapse. This replaces the older\n570\t      -- provenance (paid-pack-first) order; refund safety is now preserved a\n571\t      -- different way — refundCreditGrant clamps every clawback at the license's\n572\t      -- current active balance and reconciles against the same running total\n573\t      -- (#872 / spec §6), so neither spend nor clawback ordering can leak money\n574\t      -- regardless of which grant a given unit of usage drew from. Expired\n575\t      -- grants are excluded above and never spent.\n576\t      ORDER BY\n577\t        expires_at ASC,\n578\t        created_at ASC,\n579\t        id\n580\t      FOR UPDATE\n581\t    `);\n582\t\n583\t    let remainingToDeduct = amount;\n584\t    let deductedAmount = 0;\n585\t\n586\t    for (let index = 0; index < result.rows.length; index++) {\n587\t      if (remainingToDeduct <= 0) break;\n588\t\n589\t      const grant = result.rows[index];\n590\t      const grantRemaining = Number(grant.remaining_amount);\n591\t      const deduction = Math.min(grantRemaining, remainingToDeduct);\n592\t\n593\t      if (deduction <= 0) continue;\n594\t\n595\t      const newRemaining = grantRemaining - deduction;\n596\t      await tx\n597\t        .update(creditGrants)\n598\t        .set({\n599\t          remainingAmount: newRemaining.toString(),\n600\t          status: newRemaining === 0 ? \"spent\" : \"active\",\n601\t          updatedAt: new Date(),\n602\t        })\n603\t        .where(eq(creditGrants.id, grant.id));\n604\t\n605\t      remainingToDeduct -= deduction;\n606\t      deductedAmount += deduction;\n607\t    }\n608\t\n609\t    // Reconcile the cached balance to the authoritative grant total in the same\n610\t    // transaction (and same locked view). This both (a) reads via `tx` instead\n611\t    // of a second pooled connection, and (b) self-heals any drift between the\n612\t    // credit_balances cache and the grant rows, so the cache can never report\n613\t    // spendable credits the grants can't actually back, or vice versa.\n614\t    const balance = await reconcileCreditBalance(tx, userId);\n615\t\n616\t    return { balance, deductedAmount };\n617\t  });\n618\t}\n619\t\n620\t/**\n621\t * Atomically deduct credits from source-tracked grants, flooring at zero.\n622\t *\n623\t * Returns the new cached aggregate balance. The public API response shape is\n624\t * preserved, but the underlying grant rows record which source was consumed.\n625\t */\n626\texport async function deductCreditBalance(userId: string, amount: number): Promise<number> {\n627\t  const result = await spendCreditGrantsByProvenance(userId, amount);\n628\t  return result.balance;\n629\t}\n630\t\n631\t/**\n632\t * Spendable credit balance for a single license.\n633\t *\n634\t * The credit_grants rows are the source of truth; credit_balances is only a\n635\t * cache of their summed remaining_amount. To avoid ever reporting credits the\n636\t * user cannot actually consume (or hiding credits they can), this returns the\n637\t * authoritative grant total and lazily heals the cache when the two have\n638\t * drifted. The cheap read path (cache hit that already matches) does no write.\n639\t */\n640\texport async function getCreditBalance(userId: string): Promise<number> {\n641\t  const [cachedRow, grantsTotal] = await Promise.all([\n642\t    db.query.creditBalances.findFirst({\n643\t      where: eq(creditBalances.userId, userId),\n644\t    }),\n645\t    getActiveGrantsTotal(db, userId),\n646\t  ]);\n647\t\n648\t  const cached = cachedRow ? Number(cachedRow.balance) : null;\n649\t  if (cached !== grantsTotal) {\n650\t    // Drift detected (e.g. a legacy balance row with no matching grants, or a\n651\t    // partially-applied write): heal the cache to the authoritative total.\n652\t    await db.transaction((tx) => reconcileCreditBalance(tx, userId));\n653\t  }\n654\t\n655\t  return grantsTotal;\n656\t}\n657\t\n658\t/**\n659\t * Spendable credit balances for many accounts at once (dashboard / admin list).\n660\t *\n661\t * Like {@link getCreditBalance}, the credit_grants rows are the source of truth\n662\t * and credit_balances is only a cache. This reads the authoritative grant total\n663\t * (SUM of remaining_amount across each account's still-active grants) directly,\n664\t * so an account whose cached balance has drifted above its true grant total never\n665\t * shows phantom credits on the dashboard. Accounts with no active grants default\n666\t * to 0. (The cache still self-heals on the next single-account ledger\n667\t * operation; this read path simply never relies on it.)\n668\t */\n669\texport async function getCreditBalancesForUsers(userIds: string[]): Promise<Map<string, number>> {\n670\t  const map = new Map<string, number>();\n671\t  if (userIds.length === 0) return map;\n672\t  // Default every requested id to 0 so accounts with no active grants are\n673\t  // explicitly present in the map.\n674\t  for (const id of userIds) {\n675\t    map.set(id, 0);\n676\t  }\n677\t  const rows = await db\n678\t    .select({\n679\t      userId: creditGrants.userId,\n680\t      total: sql<string>`COALESCE(SUM(${creditGrants.remainingAmount}), 0)`,\n681\t    })\n682\t    .from(creditGrants)\n683\t    .where(\n684\t      and(\n685\t        inArray(creditGrants.userId, userIds),\n686\t        eq(creditGrants.status, \"active\"),\n687\t        sql`${creditGrants.remainingAmount} > 0`,\n688\t        sql`(${creditGrants.expiresAt} IS NULL OR ${creditGrants.expiresAt} > now())`\n689\t      )\n690\t    )\n691\t    .groupBy(creditGrants.userId);\n692\t  for (const row of rows) {\n693\t    map.set(row.userId, Number(row.total ?? 0));\n694\t  }\n695\t  return map;\n696\t}\n697\t\n698\t/**\n699\t * Attach each license's pooled account balance. Credits are pooled per account,\n700\t * so balances are resolved by the licenses' distinct owning users and every\n701\t * license of an account reports that one shared balance. Returns [] for empty\n702\t * input without issuing a query.\n703\t */\n704\tasync function attachPooledCredits<T extends { userId: string }>(\n705\t  licenses: T[]\n706\t): Promise<Array<T & { credits: number }>> {\n707\t  if (licenses.length === 0) return [];\n708\t  const balanceMap = await getCreditBalancesForUsers(\n709\t    Array.from(new Set(licenses.map((l) => l.userId)))\n710\t  );\n711\t  return licenses.map((license) => ({\n712\t    ...license,\n713\t    credits: balanceMap.get(license.userId) || 0,\n714\t  }));\n715\t}\n716\t\n717\texport interface CreditGrantHistoryRow {\n718\t  id: string;\n719\t  userId: string;\n720\t  createdAt: Date;\n721\t  expiresAt: Date | null;\n722\t  originalAmount: number;\n723\t  remainingAmount: number;\n724\t  status: string;\n725\t}\n726\t\n727\t/**\n728\t * Paid credit-pack grants for the given licenses, newest-first.\n729\t *\n730\t * Top-up history shows PAID packs only (source_type = 'stripe_credit_pack'),\n731\t * not free/included bundles or admin/legacy grants. Expired rows are still\n732\t * returned (their status/expiresAt let the UI show them as expired) — this is a\n733\t * statement of purchases, distinct from the spendable-balance reads which\n734\t * exclude expired grants.\n735\t */\n736\texport async function getPaidCreditGrantsForUsers(\n737\t  userIds: string[]\n738\t): Promise<CreditGrantHistoryRow[]> {\n739\t  if (userIds.length === 0) return [];\n740\t  const rows = await db\n741\t    .select({\n742\t      id: creditGrants.id,\n743\t      userId: creditGrants.userId,\n744\t      createdAt: creditGrants.createdAt,\n745\t      expiresAt: creditGrants.expiresAt,\n746\t      originalAmount: creditGrants.originalAmount,\n747\t      remainingAmount: creditGrants.remainingAmount,\n748\t      status: creditGrants.status,\n749\t    })\n750\t    .from(creditGrants)\n751\t    .where(\n752\t      and(\n753\t        inArray(creditGrants.userId, userIds),\n754\t        eq(creditGrants.sourceType, \"stripe_credit_pack\")\n755\t      )\n756\t    )\n757\t    .orderBy(desc(creditGrants.createdAt));\n758\t\n759\t  return rows.map((row) => ({\n760\t    id: row.id,\n761\t    userId: row.userId,\n762\t    createdAt: row.createdAt,\n763\t    expiresAt: row.expiresAt,\n764\t    originalAmount: Number(row.originalAmount),\n765\t    remainingAmount: Number(row.remainingAmount),\n766\t    status: row.status,\n767\t  }));\n768\t}\n769\t\n770\texport async function getAllAccountKeysWithCreditsForAdmin(limit = 1000): Promise<\n771\t  Array<AccountKeyRow & { credits: number }>\n772\t> {\n773\t  const licenses = await getAllAccountKeysForAdmin(limit);\n774\t  return attachPooledCredits(licenses);\n775\t}\n776\t\n777\t/**\n778\t * Fetch every license (with credit balance) belonging to the given users,\n779\t * newest-first. Used by the admin list so that a customer's full license set\n780\t * is shown even when a search only matched a subset of their licenses.\n781\t */\n782\texport async function getAccountKeysWithCreditsForUserIds(\n783\t  userIds: string[]\n784\t): Promise<Array<AccountKeyRow & { credits: number }>> {\n785\t  if (userIds.length === 0) return [];\n786\t  const rows = await db.query.accountKeys.findMany({\n787\t    where: inArray(accountKeys.userId, userIds),\n788\t    orderBy: [desc(accountKeys.createdAt)],\n789\t  });\n790\t  const licenses = rows.map(drizzleAccountKeyToRow);\n791\t  return attachPooledCredits(licenses);\n792\t}\n793\t\n794\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n795\t// Device Validations\n796\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n797\t\n798\texport async function upsertDeviceValidation(\n799\t  licenseKeyId: string,\n800\t  deviceId: string,\n801\t  deviceName?: string\n802\t): Promise<void> {\n803\t  await db\n804\t    .insert(deviceValidations)\n805\t    .values({\n806\t      licenseKeyId,\n807\t      deviceId,\n808\t      deviceName: deviceName || null,\n809\t      lastValidatedAt: new Date(),\n810\t    })\n811\t    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n812\t      target: [deviceValidations.licenseKeyId, deviceValidations.deviceId],\n813\t      set: {\n814\t        deviceName: deviceName || null,\n815\t        lastValidatedAt: new Date(),\n816\t      },\n817\t    });\n818\t}\n819\t\n820\texport async function getDevicesForLicense(\n821\t  licenseKeyId: string,\n822\t  sinceDays?: number\n823\t): Promise<\n824\t  Array<{\n825\t    deviceId: string;\n826\t    deviceName: string | null;\n827\t    createdAt: Date;\n828\t    lastValidatedAt: Date;\n829\t  }>\n830\t> {\n831\t  const conditions = [eq(deviceValidations.licenseKeyId, licenseKeyId)];\n832\t  if (sinceDays) {\n833\t    const since = new Date();\n834\t    since.setDate(since.getDate() - sinceDays);\n835\t    conditions.push(gte(deviceValidations.lastValidatedAt, since));\n836\t  }\n837\t\n838\t  const rows = await db\n839\t    .select({\n840\t      deviceId: deviceValidations.deviceId,\n841\t      deviceName: deviceValidations.deviceName,\n842\t      createdAt: deviceValidations.createdAt,\n843\t      lastValidatedAt: deviceValidations.lastValidatedAt,\n844\t    })\n845\t    .from(deviceValidations)\n846\t    .where(and(...conditions))\n847\t    .orderBy(desc(deviceValidations.lastValidatedAt));\n848\t\n849\t  return rows;\n850\t}\n851\t\n852\texport async function getDeviceCountsPerLicense(sinceDays?: number): Promise<\n853\t  Array<{\n854\t    licenseKeyId: string;\n855\t    email: string;\n856\t    licenseKey: string;\n857\t    deviceCount: number;\n858\t  }>\n859\t> {\n860\t  const conditions = [];\n861\t  if (sinceDays) {\n862\t    const since = new Date();\n863\t    since.setDate(since.getDate() - sinceDays);\n864\t    conditions.push(gte(deviceValidations.lastValidatedAt, since));\n865\t  }\n866\t\n867\t  const rows = await db\n868\t    .select({\n869\t      licenseKeyId: accountKeys.id,\n870\t      email: accountKeys.email,\n871\t      licenseKey: accountKeys.key,\n872\t      deviceCount: count(deviceValidations.id),\n873\t    })\n874\t    .from(deviceValidations)\n875\t    .innerJoin(accountKeys, eq(deviceValidations.licenseKeyId, accountKeys.id))\n876\t    .where(conditions.length > 0 ? and(...conditions) : undefined)\n877\t    .groupBy(accountKeys.id, accountKeys.email, accountKeys.key)\n878\t    .orderBy(sql`count(${deviceValidations.id}) desc`);\n879\t\n880\t  return rows.map((r) => ({ ...r, deviceCount: Number(r.deviceCount) }));\n881\t}\n882\t\n883\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n884\t// Emails\n885\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n886\t\n887\texport async function upsertEmail(data: {\n888\t  email: string;\n889\t  source?: string | null;\n890\t  ipAddress?: string | null;\n891\t  userAgent?: string | null;\n892\t  country?: string | null;\n893\t}): Promise<void> {\n894\t  await db\n895\t    .insert(emails)\n896\t    .values({\n897\t      email: data.email,\n898\t      source: data.source ?? null,\n899\t      ipAddress: data.ipAddress ?? null,\n900\t      userAgent: data.userAgent ?? null,\n901\t      country: data.country ?? null,\n902\t    })\n903\t    .onConflictDoNothing({ target: emails.email });\n904\t}\n905\t\n906\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n907\t// Sent emails (outbound audit log)\n908\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n909\t\n910\texport async function logSentEmail(data: {\n911\t  recipient: string;\n912\t  emailType: string;\n913\t  subject?: string | null;\n914\t  providerMessageId?: string | null;\n915\t  status: \"sent\" | \"failed\";\n916\t  errorMessage?: string | null;\n917\t}): Promise<void> {\n918\t  await db.insert(sentEmails).values({\n919\t    recipient: data.recipient,\n920\t    emailType: data.emailType,\n921\t    subject: data.subject ?? null,\n922\t    providerMessageId: data.providerMessageId ?? null,\n923\t    status: data.status,\n924\t    errorMessage: data.errorMessage ?? null,\n925\t  });\n926\t}\n927\t\n928\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n929\t// Users\n930\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n931\t\n932\texport async function getOrCreateUser(\n933\t  email: string,\n934\t  metadata?: { name?: string; stripeCustomerId?: string; polarCustomerId?: string }\n935\t): Promise<UserResult | null> {\n936\t  // Check if user exists\n937\t  const existing = await db.query.user.findFirst({\n938\t    where: eq(user.email, email.toLowerCase().trim()),\n939\t  });\n940\t  if (existing) return { id: existing.id, email: existing.email };\n941\t\n942\t  // Create via Better Auth's user table.\n943\t  // emailVerified stays false: this address comes from a Stripe/Polar checkout\n944\t  // or an admin/import grant, so the mailbox owner has never proven control of\n945\t  // it. The magic-link plugin flips emailVerified to true on the first\n946\t  // successful sign-in, which is the only point where ownership is verified.\n947\t  const userId = crypto.randomUUID();\n948\t  const [newUser] = await db\n949\t    .insert(user)\n950\t    .values({\n951\t      id: userId,\n952\t      name: metadata?.name ?? email.split(\"@\")[0],\n953\t      email: email.toLowerCase().trim(),\n954\t      emailVerified: false,\n955\t    })\n956\t    .returning();\n957\t  if (!newUser) return null;\n958\t\n959\t  // Also insert an account record so the user can sign in via magic link\n960\t  await db.insert(account).values({\n961\t    id: `magic-link-${userId}`,\n962\t    accountId: userId,\n963\t    providerId: \"magic-link\",\n964\t    userId: userId,\n965\t    createdAt: new Date(),\n966\t    updatedAt: new Date(),\n967\t  });\n968\t\n969\t  return { id: newUser.id, email: newUser.email };\n970\t}\n971\t\n972\texport async function getUserById(id: string): Promise<UserResult | null> {\n973\t  const existing = await db.query.user.findFirst({\n974\t    where: eq(user.id, id),\n975\t  });\n976\t  return existing ? { id: existing.id, email: existing.email } : null;\n977\t}\n978\t\n979\texport async function getUserByEmail(email: string): Promise<UserResult | null> {\n980\t  const existing = await db.query.user.findFirst({\n981\t    where: eq(user.email, email.toLowerCase().trim()),\n982\t  });\n983\t  return existing ? { id: existing.id, email: existing.email } : null;\n984\t}\n985\t\n986\texport async function getUsersByIds(ids: string[]): Promise<Map<string, UserResult>> {\n987\t  if (ids.length === 0) return new Map();\n988\t  const rows = await db.query.user.findMany({\n989\t    where: inArray(user.id, ids),\n990\t  });\n991\t  const map = new Map<string, UserResult>();\n992\t  for (const row of rows) {\n993\t    map.set(row.id, { id: row.id, email: row.email });\n994\t  }\n995\t  return map;\n996\t}\n997\t\n998\t/**\n999\t * Move a customer to a new email. Updates the user's canonical email AND every\n1000\t * license_keys.email row owned by that user, atomically, so a customer is never\n1001\t * split across two emails. The caller should pre-check uniqueness; the\n1002\t * user.email UNIQUE constraint is the final guard (a colliding write throws\n1003\t * Postgres error code 23505, which the caller maps to a conflict).\n1004\t */\n1005\texport async function updateCustomerEmail(\n1006\t  userId: string,\n1007\t  newEmail: string\n1008\t): Promise<void> {\n1009\t  const email = newEmail.toLowerCase().trim();\n1010\t  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n1011\t    // Reset emailVerified: the new address has not been proven to belong to\n1012\t    // the account holder (matches the false default for admin/import-created\n1013\t    // users). The magic-link plugin re-verifies on the next sign-in.\n1014\t    await tx\n1015\t      .update(user)\n1016\t      .set({ email, emailVerified: false, updatedAt: new Date() })\n1017\t      .where(eq(user.id, userId));\n1018\t    await tx\n1019\t      .update(accountKeys)\n1020\t      .set({ email })\n1021\t      .where(eq(accountKeys.userId, userId));\n1022\t  });\n1023\t}\n1024\t"}]},"uuid":"24f2f313-35f8-410d-ac85-88ad9f7fc1bc","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:36:08.573Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/src/lib/db-layer.ts","content":"/**\n * Database Abstraction Layer\n *\n * All database operations go through Drizzle ORM (Neon/PlanetScale).\n */\n\nimport { eq, and, desc, gte, inArray, count, sql, ilike } from \"drizzle-orm\";\nimport { generateLicenseKey } from \"@/lib/services/license-key\";\nimport { db } from \"@/src/db\";\nimport {\n  accountKeys,\n  creditBalances,\n  creditGrants,\n  deviceValidations,\n  emails,\n  sentEmails,\n  user,\n  account,\n  stripeProcessedEvents,\n} from \"@/src/db/schema\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport interface AccountKeyInsert {\n  key: string;\n  email: string;\n  userId: string;\n  status?: string;\n  polarLicenseKeyId?: string | null;\n  polarCustomerId?: string | null;\n  stripeCustomerId?: string | null;\n  stripeSessionId?: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface AccountKeyRow {\n  id: string;\n  key: string;\n  email: string;\n  userId: string;\n  status: string;\n  polarLicenseKeyId: string | null;\n  polarCustomerId: string | null;\n  stripeCustomerId: string | null;\n  stripeSessionId: string | null;\n  createdAt: Date;\n}\n\nexport type CreditGrantSourceType =\n  | \"license_bundle\"\n  | \"polar_bundle\"\n  | \"internal_bundle\"\n  | \"admin_license_bundle\"\n  | \"stripe_credit_pack\"\n  | \"admin_manual\"\n  | \"legacy_unknown\";\n\nexport interface StripeCreditGrantInsert {\n  eventId: string;\n  eventType: string;\n  stripeObjectId: string;\n  userId: string;\n  creditAmount: number;\n  sourceType?: CreditGrantSourceType;\n  sourceId?: string;\n}\n\nexport interface CreditGrantInsert {\n  userId: string;\n  amount: number;\n  sourceType: CreditGrantSourceType;\n  sourceId: string;\n}\n\nexport interface CreditGrantRefund {\n  sourceType: CreditGrantSourceType;\n  sourceId: string;\n}\n\nexport interface UserResult {\n  id: string;\n  email: string;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction drizzleAccountKeyToRow(row: typeof accountKeys.$inferSelect): AccountKeyRow {\n  return {\n    id: row.id,\n    key: row.key,\n    email: row.email,\n    userId: row.userId,\n    status: row.status,\n    polarLicenseKeyId: row.polarLicenseKeyId,\n    polarCustomerId: row.polarCustomerId,\n    stripeCustomerId: row.stripeCustomerId,\n    stripeSessionId: row.stripeSessionId,\n    createdAt: row.createdAt,\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// License Keys\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport async function insertAccountKey(data: AccountKeyInsert): Promise<AccountKeyRow | null> {\n  const polarLicenseKeyId = data.polarLicenseKeyId ?? null;\n  const insert = db\n    .insert(accountKeys)\n    .values({\n      key: data.key,\n      email: data.email,\n      userId: data.userId,\n      status: data.status ?? \"granted\",\n      polarLicenseKeyId,\n      polarCustomerId: data.polarCustomerId ?? null,\n      stripeCustomerId: data.stripeCustomerId ?? null,\n      stripeSessionId: data.stripeSessionId ?? null,\n    });\n\n  // When importing a Polar license, the unique index on polar_license_key_id\n  // (idx_license_keys_polar_license_key_id) is the authoritative dedupe guard:\n  // if a concurrent import already inserted a row for this Polar key, skip the\n  // insert and return the existing row instead of failing or creating a\n  // duplicate. The read-then-write check in importLicenseFromPolar handles the\n  // common case; this makes the rare race deterministic.\n  const [row] = polarLicenseKeyId\n    ? await insert\n        .onConflictDoNothing({ target: accountKeys.polarLicenseKeyId })\n        .returning()\n    : await insert.returning();\n\n  if (!row) {\n    return polarLicenseKeyId\n      ? await findAccountByPolarLicenseKeyId(polarLicenseKeyId)\n      : null;\n  }\n  return drizzleAccountKeyToRow(row);\n}\n\nexport async function updateAccountKey(\n  id: string,\n  updates: Partial<Pick<AccountKeyInsert, \"status\" | \"stripeCustomerId\" | \"email\">>\n): Promise<void> {\n  const values: Record<string, unknown> = {};\n  if (updates.status !== undefined) values.status = updates.status;\n  if (updates.stripeCustomerId !== undefined) values.stripeCustomerId = updates.stripeCustomerId;\n  if (updates.email !== undefined) values.email = updates.email;\n\n  await db.update(accountKeys).set(values).where(eq(accountKeys.id, id));\n}\n\nexport async function findAccountByKey(key: string): Promise<AccountKeyRow | null> {\n  const row = await db.query.accountKeys.findFirst({\n    where: eq(accountKeys.key, key.trim()),\n  });\n  return row ? drizzleAccountKeyToRow(row) : null;\n}\n\nexport async function findAccountById(id: string): Promise<AccountKeyRow | null> {\n  const row = await db.query.accountKeys.findFirst({\n    where: eq(accountKeys.id, id),\n  });\n  return row ? drizzleAccountKeyToRow(row) : null;\n}\n\nexport async function findAccountByStripeSession(sessionId: string): Promise<AccountKeyRow | null> {\n  const row = await db.query.accountKeys.findFirst({\n    where: eq(accountKeys.stripeSessionId, sessionId),\n  });\n  return row ? drizzleAccountKeyToRow(row) : null;\n}\n\nexport async function findAccountByPolarLicenseKeyId(\n  polarLicenseKeyId: string\n): Promise<AccountKeyRow | null> {\n  const row = await db.query.accountKeys.findFirst({\n    where: eq(accountKeys.polarLicenseKeyId, polarLicenseKeyId),\n  });\n  return row ? drizzleAccountKeyToRow(row) : null;\n}\n\nexport async function getAccountKeysByEmail(email: string): Promise<AccountKeyRow[]> {\n  const rows = await db.query.accountKeys.findMany({\n    where: eq(accountKeys.email, email.toLowerCase()),\n    orderBy: [desc(accountKeys.createdAt)],\n  });\n  return rows.map(drizzleAccountKeyToRow);\n}\n\nexport async function getAllAccountKeysForAdmin(limit = 1000): Promise<AccountKeyRow[]> {\n  const rows = await db.query.accountKeys.findMany({\n    orderBy: [desc(accountKeys.createdAt)],\n    limit,\n  });\n  return rows.map(drizzleAccountKeyToRow);\n}\n\n/**\n * Distinct normalized emails that hold at least one *granted* Account Key (from\n * the internal bundle or a paid purchase). Powers the ACS admin backfill, which\n * reconciles its claim flag against emails that already have a key. Read-only.\n */\nexport async function getGrantedEmails(): Promise<string[]> {\n  const rows = await db\n    .selectDistinct({ email: accountKeys.email })\n    .from(accountKeys)\n    .where(eq(accountKeys.status, \"granted\"));\n  return rows\n    .map((r) => r.email.toLowerCase().trim())\n    .filter((email) => email.length > 0);\n}\n\nexport async function searchAccountKeysByEmail(\n  email: string,\n  limit = 1000\n): Promise<Array<AccountKeyRow & { credits: number }>> {\n  const rows = await db.query.accountKeys.findMany({\n    where: ilike(accountKeys.email, `%${email}%`),\n    orderBy: [desc(accountKeys.createdAt)],\n    limit,\n  });\n  const licenses = rows.map(drizzleAccountKeyToRow);\n  return attachPooledCredits(licenses);\n}\n\n// Credits granted with each internal-bundle Account Key mint. $10 at the\n// 1000-credits-per-dollar rate. This is the ACS member perk; only brand-new\n// emails receive it (an email that already has a granted key gets no top-up).\nconst INTERNAL_BUNDLE_CREDITS = 10000;\n\n/**\n * Mint a fresh internal-bundle license for an email: generate a collision-free\n * key, ensure the user exists, insert the license as \"granted\", and grant the\n * standard 10,000-credit ($10) internal bundle.\n *\n * This is the single source of truth for the internal mint flow. Its only\n * caller is the grant-license endpoint (driven by the ACS claim flow), which\n * mints only when the email has no granted key. licenses-for-email is read-only\n * and never mints. Throws on any failure.\n */\nexport async function provisionAccountKeyForEmail(email: string): Promise<AccountKeyRow> {\n  const normalizedEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();\n\n  // Generate a unique license key with collision check\n  let key = \"\";\n  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {\n    key = generateLicenseKey();\n    const collision = await findAccountByKey(key);\n    if (!collision) break;\n    if (i === 4) {\n      throw new Error(\"Failed to generate unique license key\");\n    }\n  }\n\n  const name = normalizedEmail.split(\"@\")[0];\n  const userResult = await getOrCreateUser(normalizedEmail, { name });\n  if (!userResult) {\n    throw new Error(\"Failed to create user\");\n  }\n\n  const license = await insertAccountKey({\n    key,\n    email: normalizedEmail,\n    userId: userResult.id,\n    status: \"granted\",\n  });\n  if (!license) {\n    throw new Error(\"Failed to insert license key\");\n  }\n\n  await grantCreditLot({\n    userId: license.userId,\n    amount: INTERNAL_BUNDLE_CREDITS,\n    sourceType: \"internal_bundle\",\n    sourceId: license.id,\n  });\n\n  return license;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Credit Balances and Grants\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// A handle that can run queries: either the pooled `db` connection or a\n// transaction handle. Used so balance reads can join the caller's transaction\n// instead of opening a second connection (which sees a stale snapshot and ties\n// up the pool while row locks are held).\ntype DbExecutor = typeof db | Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0];\n\n// A grant still backs spendable balance when it has no expiry or hasn't expired\n// yet. This is the single source of that rule for the raw-SQL credit queries\n// below (balance read, spend, clawback) — keep it here so expiry semantics live\n// in one place. The Drizzle-builder variant (using creditGrants.expiresAt) is\n// expressed inline where the query is built with the query builder.\nconst ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY = sql`(expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > now())`;\n\n/**\n * Authoritative spendable balance: the SUM of remaining_amount across a\n * license's still-active grants. The credit_grants rows are the source of\n * truth; credit_balances is only a denormalized cache of this value. Reading\n * via the passed executor lets callers inside a FOR UPDATE transaction get a\n * consistent, locked view rather than a stale snapshot from the outer pool.\n */\nasync function getActiveGrantsTotal(\n  executor: DbExecutor,\n  userId: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const result = await executor.execute<{ total: string | null }>(sql`\n    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(remaining_amount), 0) AS total\n    FROM credit_grants\n    WHERE user_id = ${userId}\n      AND status = 'active'\n      AND remaining_amount > 0\n      AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n  `);\n  return Number(result.rows[0]?.total ?? 0);\n}\n\n/**\n * Force the credit_balances cache to equal the authoritative grant total,\n * reconciling any drift between the two. Returns the reconciled balance. This\n * is the single place that heals divergence (e.g. a legacy balance row with no\n * matching grants, or a partially-applied transaction) so the cache can never\n * permanently show credits the grants can't back, or vice versa.\n */\nasync function reconcileCreditBalance(\n  tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0],\n  userId: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const total = await getActiveGrantsTotal(tx, userId);\n  await tx\n    .insert(creditBalances)\n    .values({ userId, balance: total.toString() })\n    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n      target: creditBalances.userId,\n      set: {\n        balance: total.toString(),\n        updatedAt: new Date(),\n      },\n    });\n  return total;\n}\n\nasync function incrementCreditBalance(\n  tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0],\n  userId: string,\n  amount: number\n): Promise<number> {\n  const [row] = await tx\n    .insert(creditBalances)\n    .values({ userId, balance: amount.toString() })\n    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n      target: creditBalances.userId,\n      set: {\n        balance: sql`${creditBalances.balance} + ${amount}`,\n        updatedAt: new Date(),\n      },\n    })\n    .returning({ balance: creditBalances.balance });\n\n  return Number(row.balance);\n}\n\n// Every credit grant expires one year after it is created (OpenRouter model):\n// the ToS reserves the right to expire unused credits 365 days after purchase.\n// Stamped here so it covers every insert path (paid packs, minted keys, admin\n// grants); enforcement is the lazy `expires_at > now()` filter on reads/spends.\nconst CREDIT_GRANT_TTL_MS = 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n\nasync function grantCreditLotInTransaction(\n  tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0],\n  data: CreditGrantInsert\n): Promise<{ status: \"processed\" | \"duplicate\"; balance: number }> {\n  const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + CREDIT_GRANT_TTL_MS);\n  const [grantRow] = await tx\n    .insert(creditGrants)\n    .values({\n      userId: data.userId,\n      sourceType: data.sourceType,\n      sourceId: data.sourceId,\n      originalAmount: data.amount.toString(),\n      remainingAmount: data.amount.toString(),\n      refundedAmount: \"0\",\n      status: \"active\",\n      expiresAt,\n    })\n    .onConflictDoNothing({\n      target: [creditGrants.sourceType, creditGrants.sourceId],\n    })\n    .returning({ id: creditGrants.id });\n\n  if (!grantRow) {\n    // Read inside the transaction so the balance reflects the locked, in-tx\n    // view rather than a stale snapshot from a second pooled connection.\n    const balance = await getActiveGrantsTotal(tx, data.userId);\n    return { status: \"duplicate\", balance };\n  }\n\n  const balance = await incrementCreditBalance(tx, data.userId, data.amount);\n  return { status: \"processed\", balance };\n}\n\nexport async function grantCreditLot(\n  data: CreditGrantInsert\n): Promise<{ status: \"processed\" | \"duplicate\"; balance: number }> {\n  return db.transaction((tx) => grantCreditLotInTransaction(tx, data));\n}\n\nexport async function grantCreditsForStripeEvent(\n  data: StripeCreditGrantInsert\n): Promise<\"processed\" | \"duplicate\"> {\n  const insertedEvent = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    const [eventRow] = await tx\n      .insert(stripeProcessedEvents)\n      .values({\n        eventId: data.eventId,\n        eventType: data.eventType,\n        stripeObjectId: data.stripeObjectId,\n      })\n      .onConflictDoNothing({ target: stripeProcessedEvents.stripeObjectId })\n      .returning({ eventId: stripeProcessedEvents.eventId });\n\n    if (!eventRow) {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    await grantCreditLotInTransaction(tx, {\n      userId: data.userId,\n      amount: data.creditAmount,\n      sourceType: data.sourceType ?? \"stripe_credit_pack\",\n      sourceId: data.sourceId ?? data.stripeObjectId,\n    });\n\n    return eventRow;\n  });\n\n  return insertedEvent ? \"processed\" : \"duplicate\";\n}\n\nexport async function refundCreditGrant(\n  data: CreditGrantRefund\n): Promise<{ status: \"processed\" | \"duplicate\"; refundedAmount: number }> {\n  return db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    // Resolve the refunded purchase by provenance. We deliberately do NOT filter\n    // on remaining_amount > 0: a fully-consumed pack still has to be clawed back,\n    // otherwise spending the pack first (so a refund can't hand back unused paid\n    // credits — see spendCreditGrantsByProvenance / #872) would let a refund of a\n    // fully-spent pack reclaim nothing, leaving the user with the paid usage for\n    // free PLUS the cash refund (the inverse money-loss bug).\n    const target = await tx.execute<{\n      id: string;\n      user_id: string;\n      original_amount: string;\n      refunded_amount: string;\n    }>(sql`\n      SELECT id, user_id, original_amount, refunded_amount\n      FROM credit_grants\n      WHERE source_type = ${data.sourceType}\n        AND source_id = ${data.sourceId}\n      FOR UPDATE\n    `);\n\n    const grant = target.rows[0];\n    if (!grant) {\n      return { status: \"duplicate\", refundedAmount: 0 };\n    }\n\n    // The amount this refund reclaims is the grant's full purchased value that\n    // has not already been refunded. A refunded grant (refunded_amount already\n    // covers original_amount) is a no-op / duplicate webhook delivery.\n    const originalAmount = Number(grant.original_amount);\n    const alreadyRefunded = Number(grant.refunded_amount);\n    const clawback = originalAmount - alreadyRefunded;\n    if (clawback <= 0) {\n      return { status: \"duplicate\", refundedAmount: 0 };\n    }\n\n    // A full refund must remove the FULL purchased value from the license's\n    // spendable balance, regardless of which grant happened to be spent for the\n    // matching usage. Clamped at the license's current active total so a user who\n    // already burned the credits doesn't go negative, but no value escapes:\n    //   spend order + refund clawback are reconciled against the SAME running\n    //   total, so neither bundle-first nor pack-first ordering can leak money.\n    let toClawback = Math.min(\n      clawback,\n      await getActiveGrantsTotal(tx, grant.user_id)\n    );\n\n    // Draw the clawback down from the license's active, unexpired grants,\n    // starting with the refunded grant itself, then the same oldest-first order\n    // used when spending (soonest-to-expire first). The clawback total is\n    // clamped at the license's current active balance, and both spend and\n    // clawback reconcile against that same running total, so no ordering can\n    // leak money. Expired grants are skipped: they no longer back any spendable\n    // balance, so clawing them back would remove value that was never available.\n    const drawdown = await tx.execute<{ id: string; remaining_amount: string }>(sql`\n      SELECT id, remaining_amount\n      FROM credit_grants\n      WHERE user_id = ${grant.user_id}\n        AND remaining_amount > 0\n        AND status = 'active'\n        AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n      ORDER BY\n        CASE WHEN id = ${grant.id} THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,\n        expires_at ASC,\n        created_at ASC,\n        id\n      FOR UPDATE\n    `);\n\n    for (const row of drawdown.rows) {\n      if (toClawback <= 0) break;\n      const rowRemaining = Number(row.remaining_amount);\n      const deduction = Math.min(rowRemaining, toClawback);\n      if (deduction <= 0) continue;\n      const newRemaining = rowRemaining - deduction;\n      await tx\n        .update(creditGrants)\n        .set({\n          remainingAmount: newRemaining.toString(),\n          status: newRemaining === 0 ? \"spent\" : \"active\",\n          updatedAt: new Date(),\n        })\n        .where(eq(creditGrants.id, row.id));\n      toClawback -= deduction;\n    }\n\n    // Mark the refunded grant as refunded and record the full reclaimed value on\n    // it (even when the credits were physically drawn from other grants), so the\n    // grant's lifetime is correct and a duplicate refund webhook is a no-op.\n    await tx\n      .update(creditGrants)\n      .set({\n        remainingAmount: \"0\",\n        refundedAmount: (alreadyRefunded + clawback).toString(),\n        status: \"refunded\",\n        updatedAt: new Date(),\n      })\n      .where(eq(creditGrants.id, grant.id));\n\n    // Reconcile the cache to the authoritative grant total, healing any drift.\n    await reconcileCreditBalance(tx, grant.user_id);\n    return {\n      status: \"processed\",\n      refundedAmount: clawback,\n    };\n  });\n}\n\nexport async function spendCreditGrantsByProvenance(\n  userId: string,\n  amount: number\n): Promise<{ balance: number; deductedAmount: number }> {\n  return db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    const result = await tx.execute<{ id: string; remaining_amount: string }>(sql`\n      SELECT id, remaining_amount\n      FROM credit_grants\n      WHERE user_id = ${userId}\n        AND remaining_amount > 0\n        AND status = 'active'\n        AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n      -- Spend OLDEST-FIRST: soonest-to-expire grants are consumed before grants\n      -- that still have time on them (and never-expiring grants last), so a user\n      -- naturally burns down credits before they lapse. This replaces the older\n      -- provenance (paid-pack-first) order; refund safety is now preserved a\n      -- different way — refundCreditGrant clamps every clawback at the license's\n      -- current active balance and reconciles against the same running total\n      -- (#872 / spec §6), so neither spend nor clawback ordering can leak money\n      -- regardless of which grant a given unit of usage drew from. Expired\n      -- grants are excluded above and never spent.\n      ORDER BY\n        expires_at ASC,\n        created_at ASC,\n        id\n      FOR UPDATE\n    `);\n\n    let remainingToDeduct = amount;\n    let deductedAmount = 0;\n\n    for (let index = 0; index < result.rows.length; index++) {\n      if (remainingToDeduct <= 0) break;\n\n      const grant = result.rows[index];\n      const grantRemaining = Number(grant.remaining_amount);\n      const deduction = Math.min(grantRemaining, remainingToDeduct);\n\n      if (deduction <= 0) continue;\n\n      const newRemaining = grantRemaining - deduction;\n      await tx\n        .update(creditGrants)\n        .set({\n          remainingAmount: newRemaining.toString(),\n          status: newRemaining === 0 ? \"spent\" : \"active\",\n          updatedAt: new Date(),\n        })\n        .where(eq(creditGrants.id, grant.id));\n\n      remainingToDeduct -= deduction;\n      deductedAmount += deduction;\n    }\n\n    // Reconcile the cached balance to the authoritative grant total in the same\n    // transaction (and same locked view). This both (a) reads via `tx` instead\n    // of a second pooled connection, and (b) self-heals any drift between the\n    // credit_balances cache and the grant rows, so the cache can never report\n    // spendable credits the grants can't actually back, or vice versa.\n    const balance = await reconcileCreditBalance(tx, userId);\n\n    return { balance, deductedAmount };\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Atomically deduct credits from source-tracked grants, flooring at zero.\n *\n * Returns the new cached aggregate balance. The public API response shape is\n * preserved, but the underlying grant rows record which source was consumed.\n */\nexport async function deductCreditBalance(userId: string, amount: number): Promise<number> {\n  const result = await spendCreditGrantsByProvenance(userId, amount);\n  return result.balance;\n}\n\n/**\n * Spendable credit balance for a single license.\n *\n * The credit_grants rows are the source of truth; credit_balances is only a\n * cache of their summed remaining_amount. To avoid ever reporting credits the\n * user cannot actually consume (or hiding credits they can), this returns the\n * authoritative grant total and lazily heals the cache when the two have\n * drifted. The cheap read path (cache hit that already matches) does no write.\n */\nexport async function getCreditBalance(userId: string): Promise<number> {\n  const [cachedRow, grantsTotal] = await Promise.all([\n    db.query.creditBalances.findFirst({\n      where: eq(creditBalances.userId, userId),\n    }),\n    getActiveGrantsTotal(db, userId),\n  ]);\n\n  const cached = cachedRow ? Number(cachedRow.balance) : null;\n  if (cached !== grantsTotal) {\n    // Drift detected (e.g. a legacy balance row with no matching grants, or a\n    // partially-applied write): heal the cache to the authoritative total.\n    await db.transaction((tx) => reconcileCreditBalance(tx, userId));\n  }\n\n  return grantsTotal;\n}\n\n/**\n * Spendable credit balances for many accounts at once (dashboard / admin list).\n *\n * Like {@link getCreditBalance}, the credit_grants rows are the source of truth\n * and credit_balances is only a cache. This reads the authoritative grant total\n * (SUM of remaining_amount across each account's still-active grants) directly,\n * so an account whose cached balance has drifted above its true grant total never\n * shows phantom credits on the dashboard. Accounts with no active grants default\n * to 0. (The cache still self-heals on the next single-account ledger\n * operation; this read path simply never relies on it.)\n */\nexport async function getCreditBalancesForUsers(userIds: string[]): Promise<Map<string, number>> {\n  const map = new Map<string, number>();\n  if (userIds.length === 0) return map;\n  // Default every requested id to 0 so accounts with no active grants are\n  // explicitly present in the map.\n  for (const id of userIds) {\n    map.set(id, 0);\n  }\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({\n      userId: creditGrants.userId,\n      total: sql<string>`COALESCE(SUM(${creditGrants.remainingAmount}), 0)`,\n    })\n    .from(creditGrants)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        inArray(creditGrants.userId, userIds),\n        eq(creditGrants.status, \"active\"),\n        sql`${creditGrants.remainingAmount} > 0`,\n        sql`(${creditGrants.expiresAt} IS NULL OR ${creditGrants.expiresAt} > now())`\n      )\n    )\n    .groupBy(creditGrants.userId);\n  for (const row of rows) {\n    map.set(row.userId, Number(row.total ?? 0));\n  }\n  return map;\n}\n\n/**\n * Attach each license's pooled account balance. Credits are pooled per account,\n * so balances are resolved by the licenses' distinct owning users and every\n * license of an account reports that one shared balance. Returns [] for empty\n * input without issuing a query.\n */\nasync function attachPooledCredits<T extends { userId: string }>(\n  licenses: T[]\n): Promise<Array<T & { credits: number }>> {\n  if (licenses.length === 0) return [];\n  const balanceMap = await getCreditBalancesForUsers(\n    Array.from(new Set(licenses.map((l) => l.userId)))\n  );\n  return licenses.map((license) => ({\n    ...license,\n    credits: balanceMap.get(license.userId) || 0,\n  }));\n}\n\nexport interface CreditGrantHistoryRow {\n  id: string;\n  userId: string;\n  createdAt: Date;\n  expiresAt: Date | null;\n  originalAmount: number;\n  remainingAmount: number;\n  status: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Paid credit-pack grants for the given licenses, newest-first.\n *\n * Top-up history shows PAID packs only (source_type = 'stripe_credit_pack'),\n * not free/included bundles or admin/legacy grants. Expired rows are still\n * returned (their status/expiresAt let the UI show them as expired) — this is a\n * statement of purchases, distinct from the spendable-balance reads which\n * exclude expired grants.\n */\nexport async function getPaidCreditGrantsForUsers(\n  userIds: string[]\n): Promise<CreditGrantHistoryRow[]> {\n  if (userIds.length === 0) return [];\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({\n      id: creditGrants.id,\n      userId: creditGrants.userId,\n      createdAt: creditGrants.createdAt,\n      expiresAt: creditGrants.expiresAt,\n      originalAmount: creditGrants.originalAmount,\n      remainingAmount: creditGrants.remainingAmount,\n      status: creditGrants.status,\n    })\n    .from(creditGrants)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        inArray(creditGrants.userId, userIds),\n        eq(creditGrants.sourceType, \"stripe_credit_pack\")\n      )\n    )\n    .orderBy(desc(creditGrants.createdAt));\n\n  return rows.map((row) => ({\n    id: row.id,\n    userId: row.userId,\n    createdAt: row.createdAt,\n    expiresAt: row.expiresAt,\n    originalAmount: Number(row.originalAmount),\n    remainingAmount: Number(row.remainingAmount),\n    status: row.status,\n  }));\n}\n\nexport async function getAllAccountKeysWithCreditsForAdmin(limit = 1000): Promise<\n  Array<AccountKeyRow & { credits: number }>\n> {\n  const licenses = await getAllAccountKeysForAdmin(limit);\n  return attachPooledCredits(licenses);\n}\n\n/**\n * Fetch every license (with credit balance) belonging to the given users,\n * newest-first. Used by the admin list so that a customer's full license set\n * is shown even when a search only matched a subset of their licenses.\n */\nexport async function getAccountKeysWithCreditsForUserIds(\n  userIds: string[]\n): Promise<Array<AccountKeyRow & { credits: number }>> {\n  if (userIds.length === 0) return [];\n  const rows = await db.query.accountKeys.findMany({\n    where: inArray(accountKeys.userId, userIds),\n    orderBy: [desc(accountKeys.createdAt)],\n  });\n  const licenses = rows.map(drizzleAccountKeyToRow);\n  return attachPooledCredits(licenses);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device Validations\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport async function upsertDeviceValidation(\n  licenseKeyId: string,\n  deviceId: string,\n  deviceName?: string\n): Promise<void> {\n  await db\n    .insert(deviceValidations)\n    .values({\n      licenseKeyId,\n      deviceId,\n      deviceName: deviceName || null,\n      lastValidatedAt: new Date(),\n    })\n    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n      target: [deviceValidations.licenseKeyId, deviceValidations.deviceId],\n      set: {\n        deviceName: deviceName || null,\n        lastValidatedAt: new Date(),\n      },\n    });\n}\n\nexport async function getDevicesForLicense(\n  licenseKeyId: string,\n  sinceDays?: number\n): Promise<\n  Array<{\n    deviceId: string;\n    deviceName: string | null;\n    createdAt: Date;\n    lastValidatedAt: Date;\n  }>\n> {\n  const conditions = [eq(deviceValidations.licenseKeyId, licenseKeyId)];\n  if (sinceDays) {\n    const since = new Date();\n    since.setDate(since.getDate() - sinceDays);\n    conditions.push(gte(deviceValidations.lastValidatedAt, since));\n  }\n\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({\n      deviceId: deviceValidations.deviceId,\n      deviceName: deviceValidations.deviceName,\n      createdAt: deviceValidations.createdAt,\n      lastValidatedAt: deviceValidations.lastValidatedAt,\n    })\n    .from(deviceValidations)\n    .where(and(...conditions))\n    .orderBy(desc(deviceValidations.lastValidatedAt));\n\n  return rows;\n}\n\nexport async function getDeviceCountsPerLicense(sinceDays?: number): Promise<\n  Array<{\n    licenseKeyId: string;\n    email: string;\n    licenseKey: string;\n    deviceCount: number;\n  }>\n> {\n  const conditions = [];\n  if (sinceDays) {\n    const since = new Date();\n    since.setDate(since.getDate() - sinceDays);\n    conditions.push(gte(deviceValidations.lastValidatedAt, since));\n  }\n\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({\n      licenseKeyId: accountKeys.id,\n      email: accountKeys.email,\n      licenseKey: accountKeys.key,\n      deviceCount: count(deviceValidations.id),\n    })\n    .from(deviceValidations)\n    .innerJoin(accountKeys, eq(deviceValidations.licenseKeyId, accountKeys.id))\n    .where(conditions.length > 0 ? and(...conditions) : undefined)\n    .groupBy(accountKeys.id, accountKeys.email, accountKeys.key)\n    .orderBy(sql`count(${deviceValidations.id}) desc`);\n\n  return rows.map((r) => ({ ...r, deviceCount: Number(r.deviceCount) }));\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Emails\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport async function upsertEmail(data: {\n  email: string;\n  source?: string | null;\n  ipAddress?: string | null;\n  userAgent?: string | null;\n  country?: string | null;\n}): Promise<void> {\n  await db\n    .insert(emails)\n    .values({\n      email: data.email,\n      source: data.source ?? null,\n      ipAddress: data.ipAddress ?? null,\n      userAgent: data.userAgent ?? null,\n      country: data.country ?? null,\n    })\n    .onConflictDoNothing({ target: emails.email });\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Sent emails (outbound audit log)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport async function logSentEmail(data: {\n  recipient: string;\n  emailType: string;\n  subject?: string | null;\n  providerMessageId?: string | null;\n  status: \"sent\" | \"failed\";\n  errorMessage?: string | null;\n}): Promise<void> {\n  await db.insert(sentEmails).values({\n    recipient: data.recipient,\n    emailType: data.emailType,\n    subject: data.subject ?? null,\n    providerMessageId: data.providerMessageId ?? null,\n    status: data.status,\n    errorMessage: data.errorMessage ?? null,\n  });\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Users\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport async function getOrCreateUser(\n  email: string,\n  metadata?: { name?: string; stripeCustomerId?: string; polarCustomerId?: string }\n): Promise<UserResult | null> {\n  // Check if user exists\n  const existing = await db.query.user.findFirst({\n    where: eq(user.email, email.toLowerCase().trim()),\n  });\n  if (existing) return { id: existing.id, email: existing.email };\n\n  // Create via Better Auth's user table.\n  // emailVerified stays false: this address comes from a Stripe/Polar checkout\n  // or an admin/import grant, so the mailbox owner has never proven control of\n  // it. The magic-link plugin flips emailVerified to true on the first\n  // successful sign-in, which is the only point where ownership is verified.\n  const userId = crypto.randomUUID();\n  const [newUser] = await db\n    .insert(user)\n    .values({\n      id: userId,\n      name: metadata?.name ?? email.split(\"@\")[0],\n      email: email.toLowerCase().trim(),\n      emailVerified: false,\n    })\n    .returning();\n  if (!newUser) return null;\n\n  // Also insert an account record so the user can sign in via magic link\n  await db.insert(account).values({\n    id: `magic-link-${userId}`,\n    accountId: userId,\n    providerId: \"magic-link\",\n    userId: userId,\n    createdAt: new Date(),\n    updatedAt: new Date(),\n  });\n\n  return { id: newUser.id, email: newUser.email };\n}\n\nexport async function getUserById(id: string): Promise<UserResult | null> {\n  const existing = await db.query.user.findFirst({\n    where: eq(user.id, id),\n  });\n  return existing ? { id: existing.id, email: existing.email } : null;\n}\n\nexport async function getUserByEmail(email: string): Promise<UserResult | null> {\n  const existing = await db.query.user.findFirst({\n    where: eq(user.email, email.toLowerCase().trim()),\n  });\n  return existing ? { id: existing.id, email: existing.email } : null;\n}\n\nexport async function getUsersByIds(ids: string[]): Promise<Map<string, UserResult>> {\n  if (ids.length === 0) return new Map();\n  const rows = await db.query.user.findMany({\n    where: inArray(user.id, ids),\n  });\n  const map = new Map<string, UserResult>();\n  for (const row of rows) {\n    map.set(row.id, { id: row.id, email: row.email });\n  }\n  return map;\n}\n\n/**\n * Move a customer to a new email. Updates the user's canonical email AND every\n * license_keys.email row owned by that user, atomically, so a customer is never\n * split across two emails. The caller should pre-check uniqueness; the\n * user.email UNIQUE constraint is the final guard (a colliding write throws\n * Postgres error code 23505, which the caller maps to a conflict).\n */\nexport async function updateCustomerEmail(\n  userId: string,\n  newEmail: string\n): Promise<void> {\n  const email = newEmail.toLowerCase().trim();\n  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    // Reset emailVerified: the new address has not been proven to belong to\n    // the account holder (matches the false default for admin/import-created\n    // users). The magic-link plugin re-verifies on the next sign-in.\n    await tx\n      .update(user)\n      .set({ email, emailVerified: false, updatedAt: new Date() })\n      .where(eq(user.id, userId));\n    await tx\n      .update(accountKeys)\n      .set({ email })\n      .where(eq(accountKeys.userId, userId));\n  });\n}\n","numLines":1024,"startLine":1,"totalLines":1024}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e612ac82-dbd7-4417-a6c7-259127cc5dde","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}
