{"parentUuid":"621c3546-67ce-4b14-9f44-aaf36790c02e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"f0d327cc-f112-4314-af2e-21469ef6cc93","agentId":"aac5bcdb9faf85e75","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01RuKrmRvJMzycPkV4eBysy4","type":"tool_result","content":"// CREDIT VALIDATION + DEDUCTION\n// Handles preflight credit checks and post-usage deduction\n\nimport type { AuthContext } from './auth';\nimport { BYTES_PER_MINUTE_ESTIMATE, CREDITS_PER_MINUTE, DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL, LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS } from '../lib/constants';\nimport { roundToTenth, roundUpToTenth } from '../lib/utils';\nimport { creditsForCost } from '../lib/cost-calculator';\nimport { insufficientCreditsResponse } from '../lib/responses';\nimport { cacheLicense } from '../lib/redis';\n\n// Minimum encoded bitrate expected from clients for conservative credit estimation.\nconst MIN_ESTIMATED_SECONDS = 10;\n\nexport type CreditsResult =\n  | { ok: true }\n  | { ok: false; response: Response };\n\nexport interface CreditEstimateOptions {\n  costEstimators?: Array<(durationSeconds: number) => number>;\n}\n\nexport function estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes: number): number {\n  const estimatedMinutes = sizeBytes / BYTES_PER_MINUTE_ESTIMATE;\n  return Math.max(MIN_ESTIMATED_SECONDS, estimatedMinutes * 60);\n}\n\nexport function estimateCreditsFromSize(sizeBytes: number, options: CreditEstimateOptions = {}): number {\n  const estimatedSeconds = estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes);\n\n  if (options.costEstimators?.length) {\n    const maxEstimatedCost = Math.max(\n      ...options.costEstimators.map((estimateCost) => estimateCost(estimatedSeconds))\n    );\n    return Math.max(0.1, creditsForCost(maxEstimatedCost));\n  }\n\n  const estimatedCredits = (estimatedSeconds / 60) * CREDITS_PER_MINUTE;\n  return Math.max(0.1, roundUpToTenth(estimatedCredits));\n}\n\nexport async function validateCredits(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  estimatedCredits: number,\n  _clientIP: string\n): Promise<CreditsResult> {\n  const balance = roundToTenth(auth.credits);\n  if (balance < estimatedCredits) {\n    return { ok: false, response: insufficientCreditsResponse(balance, estimatedCredits) };\n  }\n  return { ok: true };\n}\n\nasync function recordLicenseUsage(\n  licenseKey: string,\n  creditsUsed: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>\n): Promise<void> {\n  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/license/credits`, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: {\n        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        license_key: licenseKey,\n        amount: creditsUsed,\n        metadata,\n      }),\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n    });\n\n    if (!response.ok) {\n      const errorData = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));\n      console.warn('POST /api/license/credits failed', {\n        status: response.status,\n        error: (errorData as Record<string, unknown>).error || 'Unknown error',\n        creditsUsed,\n      });\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const data = await response.json() as { credits_remaining?: number; credits_deducted?: number };\n    if (typeof data.credits_remaining === 'number') {\n      await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n        isValid: true,\n        credits: data.credits_remaining,\n        cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n      });\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.warn('POST /api/license/credits network error', {\n      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n    });\n  }\n}\n\n// In-flight deduction tracking for graceful shutdown.\n// Call sites fire deductCredits() without awaiting (response latency), so a\n// Fly machine recycle (SIGTERM on deploy/scale-down) between the response\n// flush and the redis/license write would silently drop the charge. Every\n// deduction registers here so the SIGTERM handler can drain before exit.\nconst inFlightDeductions = new Set<Promise<number>>();\n\nexport async function drainPendingDeductions(timeoutMs: number): Promise<number> {\n  const pendingCount = inFlightDeductions.size;\n  if (pendingCount === 0) {\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  const allSettled = Promise.allSettled([...inFlightDeductions]);\n  const timeout = new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, timeoutMs));\n  await Promise.race([allSettled, timeout]);\n  return pendingCount;\n}\n\nexport function deductCredits(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  costUsd: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n  clientIP: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const deduction = performDeduction(auth, costUsd, metadata, clientIP);\n  inFlightDeductions.add(deduction);\n  deduction\n    .catch(() => {}) // errors are logged inside performDeduction / by callers\n    .finally(() => inFlightDeductions.delete(deduction));\n  return deduction;\n}\n\nasync function performDeduction(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  costUsd: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n  _clientIP: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const creditsUsed = creditsForCost(costUsd);\n\n  if (creditsUsed <= 0) {\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  await recordLicenseUsage(auth.identifier, creditsUsed, metadata);\n  return creditsUsed;\n}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"958d35db-434a-4932-9579-26a2bc9eb46a","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:36:57.133Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"// CREDIT VALIDATION + DEDUCTION\n// Handles preflight credit checks and post-usage deduction\n\nimport type { AuthContext } from './auth';\nimport { BYTES_PER_MINUTE_ESTIMATE, CREDITS_PER_MINUTE, DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL, LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS } from '../lib/constants';\nimport { roundToTenth, roundUpToTenth } from '../lib/utils';\nimport { creditsForCost } from '../lib/cost-calculator';\nimport { insufficientCreditsResponse } from '../lib/responses';\nimport { cacheLicense } from '../lib/redis';\n\n// Minimum encoded bitrate expected from clients for conservative credit estimation.\nconst MIN_ESTIMATED_SECONDS = 10;\n\nexport type CreditsResult =\n  | { ok: true }\n  | { ok: false; response: Response };\n\nexport interface CreditEstimateOptions {\n  costEstimators?: Array<(durationSeconds: number) => number>;\n}\n\nexport function estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes: number): number {\n  const estimatedMinutes = sizeBytes / BYTES_PER_MINUTE_ESTIMATE;\n  return Math.max(MIN_ESTIMATED_SECONDS, estimatedMinutes * 60);\n}\n\nexport function estimateCreditsFromSize(sizeBytes: number, options: CreditEstimateOptions = {}): number {\n  const estimatedSeconds = estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes);\n\n  if (options.costEstimators?.length) {\n    const maxEstimatedCost = Math.max(\n      ...options.costEstimators.map((estimateCost) => estimateCost(estimatedSeconds))\n    );\n    return Math.max(0.1, creditsForCost(maxEstimatedCost));\n  }\n\n  const estimatedCredits = (estimatedSeconds / 60) * CREDITS_PER_MINUTE;\n  return Math.max(0.1, roundUpToTenth(estimatedCredits));\n}\n\nexport async function validateCredits(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  estimatedCredits: number,\n  _clientIP: string\n): Promise<CreditsResult> {\n  const balance = roundToTenth(auth.credits);\n  if (balance < estimatedCredits) {\n    return { ok: false, response: insufficientCreditsResponse(balance, estimatedCredits) };\n  }\n  return { ok: true };\n}\n\nasync function recordLicenseUsage(\n  licenseKey: string,\n  creditsUsed: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>\n): Promise<void> {\n  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/license/credits`, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: {\n        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        license_key: licenseKey,\n        amount: creditsUsed,\n        metadata,\n      }),\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n    });\n\n    if (!response.ok) {\n      const errorData = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));\n      console.warn('POST /api/license/credits failed', {\n        status: response.status,\n        error: (errorData as Record<string, unknown>).error || 'Unknown error',\n        creditsUsed,\n      });\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const data = await response.json() as { credits_remaining?: number; credits_deducted?: number };\n    if (typeof data.credits_remaining === 'number') {\n      await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n        isValid: true,\n        credits: data.credits_remaining,\n        cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n      });\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.warn('POST /api/license/credits network error', {\n      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n    });\n  }\n}\n\n// In-flight deduction tracking for graceful shutdown.\n// Call sites fire deductCredits() without awaiting (response latency), so a\n// Fly machine recycle (SIGTERM on deploy/scale-down) between the response\n// flush and the redis/license write would silently drop the charge. Every\n// deduction registers here so the SIGTERM handler can drain before exit.\nconst inFlightDeductions = new Set<Promise<number>>();\n\nexport async function drainPendingDeductions(timeoutMs: number): Promise<number> {\n  const pendingCount = inFlightDeductions.size;\n  if (pendingCount === 0) {\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  const allSettled = Promise.allSettled([...inFlightDeductions]);\n  const timeout = new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, timeoutMs));\n  await Promise.race([allSettled, timeout]);\n  return pendingCount;\n}\n\nexport function deductCredits(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  costUsd: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n  clientIP: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const deduction = performDeduction(auth, costUsd, metadata, clientIP);\n  inFlightDeductions.add(deduction);\n  deduction\n    .catch(() => {}) // errors are logged inside performDeduction / by callers\n    .finally(() => inFlightDeductions.delete(deduction));\n  return deduction;\n}\n\nasync function performDeduction(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  costUsd: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n  _clientIP: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const creditsUsed = creditsForCost(costUsd);\n\n  if (creditsUsed <= 0) {\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  await recordLicenseUsage(auth.identifier, creditsUsed, metadata);\n  return creditsUsed;\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"621c3546-67ce-4b14-9f44-aaf36790c02e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}
{"parentUuid":"98a3c92b-d6c5-4e69-ada2-1e368c40d3df","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"f0d327cc-f112-4314-af2e-21469ef6cc93","agentId":"aac5bcdb9faf85e75","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018DtP5q64CAJhEpkfzZP2sW","type":"tool_result","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useMemo, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { Card, CardBody } from \"@heroui/card\";\nimport { Button } from \"@heroui/button\";\nimport { Input } from \"@heroui/input\";\nimport { m } from \"framer-motion\";\nimport { Wallet, ShieldCheck, Layers, LogIn } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { useTranslations } from \"next-intl\";\n\nimport {\n  MIN_CREDIT_DOLLARS,\n  MAX_CREDIT_DOLLARS,\n  CREDITS_PER_DOLLAR,\n  validateCreditPurchaseAmount,\n  computeCreditPurchase,\n} from \"@/app/api/checkout/credits/validation\";\n\nconst PRESETS = [5, 10] as const;\n\nconst EMAIL_RE = /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]+$/;\n\n/**\n * Guest buy-credits UI for /credits.\n *\n * Visitor enters an email, picks a preset ($5 / $10) or a custom whole-dollar\n * amount, and we POST to /api/checkout/credits (mint path — no license key).\n * Stripe collects payment, the webhook mints a key and emails it to them.\n *\n * Copy lives under the `buyCredits` namespace in messages/*.json.\n */\nexport default function CreditsPurchase({ locale }: { locale: string }) {\n  const t = useTranslations(\"buyCredits\");\n  const [email, setEmail] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState<number>(PRESETS[0]);\n  const [customAmount, setCustomAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [isCustom, setIsCustom] = useState(false);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  const effectiveAmount = isCustom ? Number(customAmount) : amount;\n\n  // Single source of truth for bounds — same validator the API route enforces,\n  // so the client preview can't accept/reject a different amount than the server.\n  const amountValid = validateCreditPurchaseAmount(effectiveAmount) === null;\n\n  const emailValid = EMAIL_RE.test(email.trim());\n\n  const { credits, feeUsd, totalUsd } = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!amountValid) return { credits: 0, feeUsd: 0, totalUsd: 0 };\n    // Reuse the server's pricing math (cents) so the preview matches the charge.\n    const { creditAmount, creditCents, feeCents } =\n      computeCreditPurchase(effectiveAmount);\n    return {\n      credits: creditAmount,\n      feeUsd: feeCents / 100,\n      totalUsd: (creditCents + feeCents) / 100,\n    };\n  }, [amountValid, effectiveAmount]);\n\n---- admin customers grant ----\n  getUserByEmail,\n  getUsersByIds,\n  updateCustomerEmail,\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\nimport { generateLicenseKey } from \"@/lib/services/license-key\";\nimport { emailService } from \"@/lib/services/email\";\n\nconst stripe = new Stripe(process.env.STRIPE_SECRET_KEY!, {\n  apiVersion: \"2025-02-24.acacia\" as any,\n});\n\nconst MAX_ADMIN_CREDIT_GRANT = 1_000_000;\n\nexport const customersRouter = createTRPCRouter({\n  /**\n   * List customers, one row per customer (grouped by user), each carrying all\n   * of that customer's license keys. Supports optional email search filter.\n   *\n   * A search matches individual license rows, but we then expand to each\n   * matched customer's FULL license set (via their userId) so the license\n   * count, total credits, and \"moves all N licenses\" copy reflect the true\n   * totals — not just the licenses whose email matched the search term.\n   */\n  list: adminProcedure\n    .input(z.object({ search: z.string().optional() }).optional())\n    .query(async ({ input }) => {\n      try {\n        const search = input?.search?.trim();\n        const matched = search\n          ? await searchAccountKeysByEmail(search)\n          : await getAllAccountKeysWithCreditsForAdmin(1000);\n\n        // Expand search matches to each customer's full license set so counts\n        // and credits are true totals. The no-search path already has every\n        // license. Then pull canonical user.email for display.\n        const userIds = Array.from(new Set(matched.map((l) => l.userId)));\n        const licenses = search\n          ? await getAccountKeysWithCreditsForUserIds(userIds)\n          : matched;\n        const userMap = await getUsersByIds(userIds);\n\n        // Group licenses by their owning user. {"parentUuid":"942bf569-040d-449e-9f2f-faf2a9b28ce5","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"aac5bcdb9faf85e75","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdWHJNArJvWVPz34auhYU","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"[D3] is a false-positive cluster, end to end. The oracle's own caveat is exactly what happened: it flagged the **constants** but not the **functions** that consume them — because those functions do have external references. Every one of the five symbols is consumed same-file by an exported helper that the live path calls.\n\n**1. `MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT` — cap IS enforced on the live deduction path.**\n`validation.ts:14` uses it inside `validateCreditDeductionAmount`, and `route.ts:92` calls that validator *before* any DB work (line 119 `deductCreditBalance`):\n```ts\nconst amountError = validateCreditDeductionAmount(amount);\nif (amountError) return NextResponse.json({ error: amountError }, { status: 400 });\n```\nThe validator's first clause `typeof amount !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0` already rejects every hostile shape the brief asked about — non-numeric strings, `NaN`, `±Infinity` (including the `1e999` → `Infinity` JSON-parse trick, which `tests/license-credits-validation.test.ts:16` covers explicitly), `0`, and negatives. Huge values hit the `> 1_000_000` cap. Balance cannot go negative independently of this: `spendCreditGrantsByProvenance` deducts `Math.min(grantRemaining, remainingToDeduct)` per grant, so it floors at 0 structurally.\n\nOn authentication: the route is indeed authenticated by the license key alone (no shared secret) — `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/middleware/credits.ts` POSTs `{license_key, amount, metadata}` with no auth header. So a desktop-client user holding a key can craft a body directly. But the blast radius is self-inflicted and bounded (≤1M per call, floored at 0), and reaching another account requires already knowing their key, which in this design *is* their wallet. No cross-account path.\n\n**2. `CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE` — no divergent implementation exists.** Used at `validation.ts:18` (`Number(amount.toFixed(CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE)) !== amount`). Grepping for an inlined `toFixed(2)` on a money path turned up only UI formatting (`CreditsPurchase.tsx:234`, MB display in `older-versions/page.tsx`) — no second rounding implementation. I also checked the reverse risk (guard applied, live caller violates it): the cloud sends `creditsForCost()` → `roundUpToTenth()` = `Math.ceil(x*10)/10`, i.e. one decimal place, which round-trips through `toFixed(2)` cleanly. The guard never spuriously 400s a real deduction.\n\n**3. `CREDIT_FEE_RATE` — no mischarge; one number, three consumers.** `validation.ts:50` is the only place fee cents are computed (`Math.round(creditCents * CREDIT_FEE_RATE)`), and all three surfaces call `computeCreditPurchase`: the route (`route.ts:51` → `unit_amount: feeCents` at :182), the client preview (`CreditsPurchase.tsx:52`, with a comment saying so), and the tests (`credit-purchase-validation.test.ts:57-79`). The only \"6%\" literals anywhere are human-readable strings — a Stripe line-item label, a doc comment, and the ToS — never arithmetic. Cosmetic drift risk if the rate ever changes, not a bug.\n\n**4. `spendCreditGrantsByProvenance` — it IS the live path.** `db-layer.ts:627`, inside `deductCreditBalance`, which `route.ts:119` calls. There is no second, competing spend implementation. Its ordering is deliberate and documented: `ORDER BY expires_at ASC, created_at ASC, id` (soonest-to-expire first), replacing an older provenance/paid-pack-first order, with refund safety re-established via `refundCreditGrant` clamping every clawback at the account's current active total (`db-layer.ts:489`, referencing #872). The name is now a misnomer — it no longer orders by provenance — which is probably what drew the oracle's eye, but the behaviour is correct and expiry-first is the *right* order for preserving user value.\n\n**5. `getAllAccountKeysForAdmin` — used same-file, admin capability intact.** `db-layer.ts:773` inside `getAllAccountKeysWithCreditsForAdmin`, which `server/api/routers/admin/customers.ts:55` calls from the admin customer-list tRPC procedure. Nothing missing.\n\nOne footnote, not a finding: `route.ts:136` returns `credits_deducted: amount` (the *requested* amount) while `spendCreditGrantsByProvenance` computes a real `deductedAmount` that `deductCreditBalance` (`db-layer.ts:627-628`) discards. On an overdraw these differ. It cannot produce a wrong balance — the balance comes from `reconcileCreditBalance` against the authoritative grant rows — and the sole consumer (`recordLicenseUsage`) reads only `credits_remaining`. It makes the route's `console.log` audit line inaccurate in an overdraw, which upstream `validateCredits` already prevents. Not worth a candidate block.\n\nBENIGN: `MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT` (nextjs/app/api/license/credits/validation.ts:1) — consumed at line 14 by `validateCreditDeductionAmount`, which `nextjs/app/api/license/credits/route.ts:92` calls before `deductCreditBalance` at line 119; cap, finiteness, positivity and type are all enforced on the live POST path.\nBENIGN: `CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE` (nextjs/app/api/license/credits/validation.ts:7) — consumed at line 18 (`Number(amount.toFixed(CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE)) !== amount`) in the same enforced validator; no inlined rounding literal exists on any money path, and the cloud's `roundUpToTenth` output satisfies it.\nBENIGN: `CREDIT_FEE_RATE` (nextjs/app/api/checkout/credits/validation.ts:13) — consumed at line 50 by `computeCreditPurchase`, the single fee-math implementation, called by the route (line 51), the client preview, and the tests; Your three independent conclusions match mine exactly; concentrating below on the two you flagged as open.\n\n**4. `spendCreditGrantsByProvenance` — it IS the live path; nothing spends grants instead.**\n`nextjs/src/lib/db-layer.ts:627`, inside `deductCreditBalance`:\n```ts\nexport async function deductCreditBalance(userId: string, amount: number): Promise<number> {\n  const result = await spendCreditGrantsByProvenance(userId, amount);\n  return result.balance;\n}\n```\nand `nextjs/app/api/license/credits/route.ts:119` calls `deductCreditBalance`. A repo-wide grep for a competing spend implementation found only three hits for the symbol: the definition (555), that caller (627), and a comment reference (452). There is no second drawdown loop anywhere.\n\nExpiry ordering is respected — it's the *only* implementation, so there's nothing to diverge from, and the order it uses is the value-preserving one:\n```sql\nORDER BY expires_at ASC, created_at ASC, id\nFOR UPDATE\n```\nSoonest-to-expire first, so a user burns credits down before they lapse. The in-file comment records that this deliberately *replaced* an older provenance (paid-pack-first) order, with refund safety re-established differently: `refundCreditGrant` clamps every clawback at the account's current active total (`db-layer.ts:489`) and both spend and clawback reconcile against that same running total (#872), so no ordering can leak money. The function name is now a misnomer — it no longer orders by provenance — which I suspect is what drew the oracle's eye, but the behaviour is correct and strictly better for the user than the name implies.\n\n**5. `getAllAccountKeysForAdmin` — used same-file; no admin capability missing.**\n`db-layer.ts:773`, inside `getAllAccountKeysWithCreditsForAdmin`, which is called externally at `nextjs/server/api/routers/admin/customers.ts:55` from the admin customer-list tRPC procedure (`: await getAllAccountKeysWithCreditsForAdmin(1000)`). The raw variant is just the un-enriched inner query; the credits-attached wrapper is what admin uses.\n\nTwo extra confirmations on your three, in case they're useful for the writeup: both `validation.ts` files have dedicated tests that import the constants directly (`nextjs/tests/license-credits-validation.test.ts`, `nextjs/tests/credit-purchase-validation.test.ts`) — including a `JSON.parse('{\"amount\":1e999}')` → `Infinity` case. And I checked the reverse risk on `CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE` (guard applied but live caller violates it, which would 400 every deduction and give free transcription): the cloud sends `creditsForCost()` → `roundUpToTenth()` = `Math.ceil(x*10)/10`, one decimal place, which round-trips through `toFixed(2)` cleanly. No spurious rejections.\n\nNon-finding worth one line for the record: `route.ts:136` returns `credits_deducted: amount` (requested) while `spendCreditGrantsByProvenance` computes a real `deductedAmount` that `deductCreditBalance` discards. These differ only on an overdraw, which upstream `validateCredits` prevents; the sole consumer (`recordLicenseUsage`) reads only `credits_remaining`; and the balance itself is authoritative via `reconcileCreditBalance`. Log-cosmetic, not a defect — I did not raise it as a candidate.\n\nFinal blocks, verbatim:\n\nBENIGN: `MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT` (nextjs/app/api/license/credits/validation.ts:1) — consumed at line 14 by `validateCreditDeductionAmount`, which nextjs/app/api/license/credits/route.ts:92 calls before `deductCreditBalance` at line 119; cap, finiteness, positivity and type are all enforced on the live POST path.\nBENIGN: `CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE` (nextjs/app/api/license/credits/validation.ts:7) — consumed at line 18 (`Number(amount.toFixed(CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE)) !== amount`) in that same enforced validator; no inlined rounding literal exists on any money path, and the cloud's `roundUpToTenth` output satisfies it.\nBENIGN: `CREDIT_FEE_RATE` (nextjs/app/api/checkout/credits/validation.ts:13) — consumed at line 50 by `computeCreditPurchase`, the single fee-math implementation, called by the route (line 51), the client preview (components/credits/CreditsPurchase.tsx:52), and the tests; every other \"6%\" in the tree is display copy, not arithmetic.\nBENIGN: `spendCreditGrantsByProvenance` (nextjs/src/lib/db-layer.ts:555) — called at line 627 by `deductCreditBalance`, the function the credits route invokes; it is the only spend implementation and correctly spends soonest-to-expire grants first.\nBENIGN: `getAllAccountKeysForAdmin` (nextjs/src/lib/db-layer.ts:194) — called at line 773 by `getAllAccountKeysWithCreditsForAdmin`, used by the admin customers router at nextjs/server/api/routers/admin/customers.ts:55.\n\nWorking tree untouched; no DB or network calls made.","type":"message","recipient":"oracle-hunter","content":"[D3] analysis complete — no candidates. It doesn't: the cloud sends `roundUpToTenth()` output, one decimal place, which round-trips through `toFixed(2)` cleanly.\n\nOn the severity question in the brief: `/api/license/credits` is authenticated by the license key in the body alone, no shared secret, so a desktop-client user can craft requests directly. But the blast radius is self-inflicted and bounded — the validator rejects `NaN`/`±Infinity`/negatives/non-numerics/zero before any DB work, caps at 1M, and `spendCreditGrantsByProvenance` floors at 0 structurally via `Math.min(grantRemaining, remainingToDeduct)`.
